On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [220] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过在ARM LX2处理器上利用SME技术优化SPECFEM3D，不仅实现了高达6倍的性能飞跃，还重新定义了谱元法在现代ARM架构上的最佳离散化参数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的谱元法（SEM）实现无法充分发挥新型ARM多核CPU中可扩展矩阵扩展（SME）架构的性能潜力。

Method: 1.开发了适用于SME的批量小矩阵内核，优化张量积运算；2.设计了针对有限高带宽内存（HBM）系统的混合MPI+OpenMP执行策略；3.进行了基于色散理论的(h,p)离散化权衡分析。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数下，该优化方案相较于原代码实现了4--6倍的性能提升，且优于非SME CPU基准测试，同时使得性能最优的操作点向更高阶多项式偏移。

Conclusion: SME不仅能显著提升谱元法（SEM）的核心算子效率，还能改变高性能计算中的离散化权衡，通过支持更高多项式阶数，进一步降低求解时间和工作集大小。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [221] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Maestro 是一款专为 LLM 多智能体系统设计的资源感知调度器，通过预测各阶段负载需求，在节点、集群和全局层面实施动态优化，从而在严格的 GPU 预算下大幅降低内存占用并提升 SLA 达标率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-MAS 虽然能处理复杂任务，但其多轮交互特性导致资源消耗激增。现存系统在大规模部署时面临解码阶段成本不确定、内存碎片化、资源过度配置以及跨集群调度等挑战，导致在有 GPU 预算限制的云环境中难以高效运行。

Method: Maestro 提出了一种工作负载感知（workload-aware）的层级化调度方案：包括：1. 预测机制：预测各阶段输出长度和内存需求；2. 节点级优化：通过层级权重缓存（hierarchical weight caching）和弹性内存供应实现多模型共存；3. 集群级/全局级调度：通过延迟感知路由（latency-aware routing）和工作流感知的优先级控制（workflow-aware prioritization）来优化资源分配和减少队头阻塞。

Result: 在实验中，Maestro 将 KV 缓存占用的 HBM 降低了 67.2%，在高争用场景下的 SLO 达成率较传统 EDF 调度算法提升了 23.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro 是一个针对资源受限云环境的 LLM-MAS 调度系统。通过结合负载感知预测与层级化调度策略，有效地平衡了 GPU 资源利用率与系统服务质量（SLO），证明了利用代理语义和负载特征可以显著提升多 Agent 协作工作流的系统效率。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks. Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [222] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用Web3技术增强联邦学习透明度和抗攻击性的分层框架，通过共识机制和声誉系统解决了传统FL中聚合过程不透明和依赖外部验证器的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习系统存在聚合过程不透明、审计困难的问题，且现行缓解措施依赖额外的外部验证器，导致了显著的计算与通信开销。

Method: 提出了一种分层联邦学习架构，利用Web3技术实现去中心化；通过委派管理器负责协调；引入新颖性检测（Novelty Detection）与共识机制处理模型更新；采用基于声誉评分的备份机制保障模型生成。

Result: 通过在真实场景下的实验验证，该框架在有效性、抗攻击性（对抗及投毒攻击）方面表现优异，证明了系统在提升透明度和信任度方面的能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的联邦学习框架通过结合Web3技术，成功在无需额外外部验证器的情况下，实现了联邦学习的高透明度、高可审计性及对恶意攻击的强鲁棒性，具备在非企业环境中广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [223] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DMR框架，实现了生产环境下非侵入式的MPI应用弹性管理，在保持性能水平的同时显著降低了HPC作业的资源成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC集群多依赖固定的资源分配方式，虽然许多科学应用具备时变行为，但现有的动态资源管理技术大多停留在实验室阶段，缺乏在实际生产环境中的落地应用。

Method: 提出了DMR（Dynamic Management of Resources）框架，采用非侵入式方法，无需修改调度器或对应用程序进行大规模代码重构即可实现MPI作业的弹性扩展。

Result: 在三个TOP500超算集群上对两个大规模科学应用进行了测试，结果显示该方法在实现与静态基准相当的性能的同时，显著降低了节点小时数（node-hour）的资源消耗。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在生产级HPC系统中实现资源弹性（Malleability）是切实可行的，能够通过非侵入式手段有效降低计算资源开销，为动态资源管理在超算环境中的广泛应用扫清了障碍。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>


### [224] [Skiplists with Foresight: Skipping Cache Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13321)
*Tomer Cory,Niv Sulimany,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Foresight 是一种缓存友好的跳表优化技术，通过提升缓存局部性，显著提高了单线程及并发跳表的吞吐量及数据库的系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳表作为广泛应用的索引数据结构，其性能受限于缓存友好性，尤其是在并发场景下，同步开销与缓存效率之间的平衡是提升性能的关键。

Method: 提出 Foresight 优化技术，并针对其在并发环境下的同步挑战提出了解决方案；将其应用于多种现有的顺序和并发跳表设计，并在 DBx1000 内存数据库中进行了系统级评估。

Result: 在微基准测试中，吞吐量提升最高可达 45%；在 DBx1000 内存数据库应用中，端到端性能提升最高可达 15%。

Conclusion: Foresight 是一种轻量级且易于集成的跳表优化方案，通过提升缓存友好性，在单线程和多线程场景下均能显著提升性能。

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [225] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对卫星星座等稀疏网络拓扑环境，论文提出了一种“邻居窃取”工作调度策略，通过限制窃取范围降低网络通信延迟，实验证明该策略在保持高效负载均衡的同时，比全局窃取更具可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步多任务（AMT）模型主要针对网络全连接、低延迟的HPC环境，通过全局工作窃取实现负载均衡；而空间边缘计算（SEC）环境中的卫星星座具有高延迟和拓扑稀疏（多跳）特性，直接应用全局窃取会导致性能瓶颈，因此需要一种适应稀疏网络拓扑的工作调度策略。

Method: 提出了一种“仅限邻居”（neighbor-only）的工作窃取策略；构建分析模型评估该策略在不同星座规模下的延迟优势；在HPC集群上通过仿真稀疏网格拓扑进行实验，对比了该策略与传统全局窃取策略在负载均衡上的表现。

Result: 理论模型显示邻居窃取具有显著的延迟优势；在HPC仿真实验中，邻居窃取策略在平衡和不平衡工作负载下，性能仅比全局窃取方案低约2.2%，表明其在不损耗负载均衡效果的前提下，更能适应高延迟的稀疏网络。

Conclusion: 实验表明，邻居窃取策略在负载均衡性能上与全局窃取策略相当，且随着系统规模的扩大，其低延迟优势使其成为更具潜力的选择，证明了在具有稀疏网格拓扑的计算环境中（如星座边缘计算）采用该策略的可行性。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [226] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了“时间导纳”（temporal conductance）概念，解决了静态导纳在动态网络中无法处理断连快照的问题，并给出并证明了选民模型在动态网络中的期望共识时间最优界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态导纳在动态网络中，一旦某些快照断开连接就会趋向于0，无法有效衡量跨时间维度的连通性对信息扩散的影响，因此需要一种更鲁棒、更具普适性的连通性度量标准。

Method: 提出了一个新的连通性度量指标“时间导纳”（temporal conductance, Φ），该指标能够捕捉跨越不同时间步出现的边所提供的连通性；推广了Berenbrink等人在静态网络上的理论边界，证明了标准选民模型在动态网络中的期望共识时间上界为 O(m/(d_minΦ))。

Result: 证明了标准选民模型在动态网络上的期望共识时间为 O(m/(d_minΦ))，且该边界在常数因子内是紧致的。

Conclusion: 作者引入了“时间导纳”（temporal conductance）这一概念，成功将其应用于分析动态网络中选民模型的收敛时间，并证明了该界限在常数因子内是紧致的。该指标有望成为分析时间网络和其他非齐次马尔可夫链的有效工具。

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [227] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对传统 Future 模型无法进行原地内存修改的局限，本文提出了 await_delete 机制以支持内存重用，并成功将其应用于高性能稠密矩阵求逆任务，证明了在大规模计算下该方法具有良好的并行扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Future 模型虽有助于处理复杂的细粒度数据依赖，但其传统的“值不可变”特性限制了高性能线性代数所需的内存原地更新（In-place update）与内存回收，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 1. 引入了新的编程构造 await_delete，允许在消费者使用完毕后安全地重用 Future 所占用的内存；2. 基于此开发了一种用于稠密对称矩阵分块求逆的 Future 算法；3. 在 Taskflow 框架上进行扩展实现，并进行强扩展性实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，尽管 Future 对小规模问题存在显著开销，但在大规模矩阵计算中表现出了近乎线性的扩展性，并分析了该方法的性能摊销阈值。

Conclusion: Futures 结合 await_delete 机制是处理大规模线性代数计算的一种可行且高效的工具，尤其在矩阵规模较大时，通过在任务调度中引入内存重用机制，能够实现近乎线性的扩展性。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Non-Parametric Dual-Manifold Mapping via 8-Bit Bounded Transformation Matrices: Challenging FP-centric Hardware Paradigms in Low-Energy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13328)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需8位整数算术的训练免除计算框架，通过双流形映射实现高效AI推理，大幅降低了计算与功耗开销，并展现出极高的系统鲁棒性，旨在重塑边缘AI计算的硬件范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现代深度学习硬件过度依赖算力密集型浮点算术（FP32/FP16/FP8）带来的高能耗与高热负载问题，探索更低成本、更高效的神经拟态边缘计算路径。

Method: 提出了一种在8位有符号整数范围内运行的双流形映射框架，使用空间流形（8192神经元）和Gabor池化结构流形（4096神经元）通过Z矩阵进行变换；推理过程基于缓存友好的指针偏移、位掩码及方向符号电荷累积；学习过程采用限制在[-127, 127]范围内的局部边界更新机制，并辅以随机噪声注入。

Result: 该框架在90%的截断稀疏度和20%的随机节点破坏下，依然保持了近乎完美的重构能力，表现出极强的全息韧性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种非参数化、无需训练的双流形映射框架，通过8位整数运算替代昂贵的浮点运算，证明了在边缘计算场景下，基于比特位运算和累加逻辑的方案足以实现高效且具有极强鲁棒性的AI推理，挑战了当前依赖GPU浮点运算的硬件范式。

Abstract: Modern deep learning hardware paradigms rely heavily on computationally expensive floating-point arithmetic (FP32, FP16, and FP8), requiring massive thermal and energetic overheads to maintain gradient-based optimization. This paper introduces a non-parametric, training-free computational framework for dual-manifold mapping that operates strictly within an 8-bit signed integer boundary and leverages simple bitwise and accumulation logic. By mapping a Spatial Manifold (N_spatial = 8192 neurons) and a Gabor-pooled Structural Manifold (N_structural = 4096 neurons) through an integer-based transformation matrix (Z-matrix), we eliminate the need for floating-point multipliers. Inference is achieved via cache-friendly pointer offsets and bitwise masks, accumulating directional sign-charges using fixed thresholds (theta_reject = 8.0, theta_cut = 2.0). Learning is executed through a localized, bounded update mechanism restricted strictly within [-127, 127], modulated by stochastic noise injection. Both architectures demonstrate extreme holographic resilience, preserving near-perfect reconstruction via a global scaling factor under 90% truncation sparsity and 20% random node destruction. By reducing core AI inference to 8-bit boundaries and boolean-like execution, this framework outlines a paradigm shift toward neuromorphic edge-computing, directly questioning the long-term necessity of dense, floating-point-centric GPU accelerators.

</details>


### [229] [SupraSNN: Exploiting Synapse-Level Parallelism in Spiking Neural Network Accelerators through Co-Optimized Mapping and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13354)
*Seyed Sadra Ghavami,Mohammad Hossein Nikkhah,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SupraSNN 借鉴超标量处理器设计，通过解耦突触与神经元计算实现了极高突触级并行度，大幅提升了 SNN 在 FPGA 上的推理性能与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲神经网络（SNN）因难以高效管理和执行大规模并行突触计算而受限于硬件部署，缺乏类似传统处理器中“超标量架构”的高效并行机制。

Method: 提出了一种软硬件协同设计的 SupraSNN 架构，采用“多播树”分发脉冲、“突触处理单元”并行计算，以及“归并树”集中处理神经元状态；配合一套包含分区与启发式调度的框架，实现高效的任务映射与执行。

Result: 在 MNIST 数据集上实现 149 μs 延迟和 0.025 mJ/图像的能耗（较现有方案能效提升 5.6 倍）；在 Spiking Heidelberg 数据集上实现 1.41 ms 延迟和 0.77 mJ/样本的能耗。

Conclusion: SupraSNN 通过解耦突触与神经元计算并引入超标量架构理念，成功解决了 SNN 在硬件高效并行执行上的瓶颈，在延迟和能效方面均显著优于现有 FPGA 加速器方案。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a brain-inspired path toward highly efficient computation, but their practical deployment is constrained by the challenge of managing and executing their massive parallelism on physical hardware. This problem mirrors the historical challenge in processor design of moving beyond serial execution, a barrier broken by superscalar architectures that dispatch multiple instructions to parallel functional units. Drawing inspiration from this paradigm, we introduce a hardware-software co-design framework that treats synaptic events as parallelizable micro-operations. We present SupraSNN, a superscalar-inspired architecture that achieves high synapse-level parallelism by physically decoupling synaptic and neuronal computations. Within this architecture, a Multi-Cast Tree routes spike data to multiple parallel Synapse Processing Units serve as the computational pipelines, while a Merge Tree consolidates distributed results for processing by a unified Neuron Unit--deliberately centralizing complex neuron state dynamics to mitigate hardware overhead and resource duplication. The efficacy of this architecture is enabled by a sophisticated partitioning and scheduling framework that first maps the SNN onto hardware respecting memory constraints, then heuristic scheduling determines the synaptic execution order, maximizing throughput and resource utilization. Implementing a feedforward SNN trained on MNIST (93.44% accuracy), SupraSNN achieves 149 $μs$ inference latency and 0.025 mJ per image (0.276 nJ per synapse) on the Xilinx Zynq XC7Z020 FPGA--delivering 47.6% lower latency and 5.6$\times$ better energy efficiency than prior FPGA-based SNN accelerators. Beyond vision tasks, a recurrent SNN on the Spiking Heidelberg Dataset (71.82% accuracy) achieves 1.41 ms latency and 0.77 mJ per sample on XC7Z030.

</details>


### [230] [ReSCom: A Reconfigurable Spiking Neural Network Accelerator Using Stochastic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13560)
*Ali Alipour Fereidani,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ReSCom 是一款利用随机计算优化脉冲神经网络（SNN）的硬件加速器，在保证推理稳定性的前提下，实现了高能效比，并支持在运行时灵活调整模型精度与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络虽然具有能效潜力，但在硬件实现中面临神经元计算带来的高功耗与大面积占用问题，且传统的近似算术容易导致循环状态更新的不稳定。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ReSCom 的可重构 SNN 加速器架构：使用随机计算（Stochastic Computing）处理神经元动力学中的乘法运算以降低复杂度，同时保留定点算术处理加/减法以保证稳定性；支持多种神经元模型（IF, LIF, Synaptic）的统一可重构设计；并支持通过调整随机位流长度（bit-stream length）进行精度与能耗的动态权衡。

Result: 在 Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA 上的 MNIST 推理任务中实现了 92.80% 的分类准确率，运行频率为 100 MHz 时，单张图像的处理能耗仅为 0.05 mJ，性能优于当前主流的 SNN 硬件实现。

Conclusion: ReSCom 为脉冲神经网络（SNN）提供了一种高效、可重构的硬件加速方案。通过结合随机计算与定点运算，该架构不仅降低了硬件资源消耗，还实现了在运行时对精度、延迟和能耗的动态平衡调整，是面向低功耗 SNN 推理的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) provide an attractive framework for energy-efficient inference due to their event-driven computation and biologically inspired dynamics. However, efficient hardware realization of SNNs remains challenging because neuronal computations incur significant power and area costs, and uncontrolled approximate arithmetic can destabilize recurrent state updates when precision is not properly managed. To address these challenges, this paper presents ReSCom, a reconfigurable SNN accelerator that leverages stochastic computing to reduce hardware complexity while maintaining stable inference. The proposed architecture employs stochastic arithmetic for multiplication operations in neuron dynamics, while preserving exact fixed-point addition/subtraction operations. This stochastic strategy enables runtime trade-offs between accuracy, latency, and energy consumption. A unified reconfigurable neuron design supports Integrate-and-Fire (IF), Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF), and Synaptic neuron models within a single hardware framework. Experimental results for MNIST inference on a Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA show that ReSCom achieves $92.80\%$ classification accuracy while consuming just $0.05~\mathrm{mJ}$ of operational energy per image at $100~\mathrm{MHz}$, outperforming the energy efficiency of recent state-of-the-art implementations. Furthermore, managing the stochastic bit-stream length allows explicit, dynamic control over accuracy-latency-energy trade-offs to meet target application constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 LLM 幻觉检测建模为最快变化检测问题，指出分类指标掩盖了检测延迟；实验表明，通过序贯分析方法，当前模型即使在改进后仍距离最优检测极限存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉检测多被评估为分类任务（AUC），但这忽视了在线监控场景中关键的“响应时间”（即从幻觉发生到报警的滞后令牌数），导致评估指标与实际应用需求脱节。

Method: 1. 将幻觉检测建模为“最快变化检测问题”（Quickest Change Detection）；2. 构建潜变量 faithful/hallucinated 的一阶马尔可夫模型；3. 使用因果循环标签器（Causal Recurrent Labeler）作为带有学习增量的 CUSUM；4. 引入基于 Donsker-Varadhan 类型的最优信息率定理进行分析。

Result: 1. 理论下界：在 0.01 的误报率下，理论检测延迟为 1.3 个令牌；2. 实际性能：因果循环标签器在 11-13 个令牌内检测，优于线性基准（31 个令牌）；3. 性能缺口：现有学习评分仅实现了特征携带有信息散度（divergence）的 1/4.5，存在显著的非效率，且受到有限时域效应的影响。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了幻觉检测的延迟结构，而使用序贯分析（Sequential Analysis）可以使这一延迟可测量。当前基于学习的方法在检测效率上仍有较大空间，且其优势主要源于单令牌得分的准确性，而非时间序列的累积。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [232] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于伊辛模型的玻尔兹曼注意力机制，通过引入位置间的可学习交互，超越了传统Softmax注意力，并验证了其在长序列建模中的优势及利用量子退火训练的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Softmax注意力机制主要依赖单个查询-键对的相似度，缺乏对注意力决策之间显式交互（如协同或拮抗关系）的参数化建模能力，限制了其处理复杂长程依赖的性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于能量模型的玻尔兹曼注意力机制，将注意力模式建模为相互作用的伊辛模型（Ising model），通过可学习的成对耦合项增强了标准的点积注意力。同时，引入绝热量子退火作为一种实用的训练策略。

Result: 在字符级语言模型和合成括号匹配任务中，玻尔兹曼注意力在Transformer架构中能够持续优于标准Softmax注意力，且随序列长度增加优势更明显；消融实验证实性能提升源于可学习的成对耦合。

Conclusion: 玻尔兹曼注意力机制（Boltzmann attention）通过引入显式的成对耦合，有效增强了Transformer对序列内位置间交互（协同或拮抗）的建模能力，且该框架为利用量子退火进行训练提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [233] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCal 是一种用于 LLM 路由的强化学习奖励校准框架，通过层次化奖励分解和分布感知优化，解决了多目标奖励产生的信用模糊与优化偏见问题，从而提高了训练效率和模型路由精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 路由强化学习方法在面对复杂任务时存在“奖励聚合导致的信用分配模糊”以及“奖励分布差异带来的优化偏差（偏向简单样本）”等问题，导致模型难以获取有效且可比的学习信号。

Method: 提出 ReCal 框架：1. 层次化奖励分解机制（Hierarchical reward decomposition）及组件级优势估计；2. 分布感知优化策略，包括方差感知重加权（variance-aware reweighting）和数据集内归一化（per-dataset normalization）。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，ReCal 在路由性能和训练稳定性方面均显著优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: ReCal 框架通过细粒度的奖励分解和分布感知的优化策略，有效克服了传统强化学习路由方法中信号模糊和优化偏差的问题，提升了路由性能与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [234] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T2SP 是一种将时间序列转化为结构化代码的表征方法，它通过将时序结构提取提前至预处理阶段，使现成的 LLM 能够更高效、准确地处理时间序列任务，且无需进行额外的模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 处理时间序列的方法（如数值序列直接序列化或微调）存在模态不匹配问题，导致 LLM 在长序列任务中性能下降且计算开销巨大。因此，需要一种更适合 LLM 理解的表征方式。

Method: 提出了 Time-Series-to-Structured-Program (T2SP) 表示法。这是一种确定性的、无需训练的方法，它将原始时间序列分解为趋势、周期和重要事件，并将其编码为与 LLM 训练语料兼容的结构化程序格式。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三类推理任务中，T2SP 相比于传统的原始字符串表示，在提升性能、减少推理时间和降低故障率方面表现出一致的优势。

Conclusion: T2SP 是一种高效的训练无关接口，它通过将时间序列转化为符号化程序，成功克服了 LLM 在处理数值序列时的模态不匹配问题，显著提升了 LLM 在时间序列任务中的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [235] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决由于矩阵求逆导致的克里斯托弗函数维度扩张问题，本文提出了 UCF，一种基于平方距离的单变量方法，有效实现了高效、精准的异常检测，并在多个基准测试中优于现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于克里斯托弗函数的异常检测方法虽具有坚实的数学基础，但受限于维度灾难，即在处理中高维数据时，矩阵求逆的计算复杂度呈指数级增长，难以实际应用。

Method: 提出了一种单变量克里斯托弗函数（UCF），该方法基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离，避开了传统方法中在大维度数据下指数级增长的矩阵求逆计算。

Result: 在 ADBench 基准测试中，UCF 在平均精度（Average Precision）方面表现优异，持续超越了 14 个 SOTA（当前最优）基准模型。

Conclusion: UCF 通过简化克里斯托弗函数（Christoffel function）的计算方式，在保持良好理论特性的同时，成功克服了高维数据下的计算瓶颈，并为异常检测领域提供了一种高效、稳健且具理论基础的新方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [236] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对忆阻器计算中ADC精度丢失的优化方案，通过层级权重分配调节或移除编码线性变换，在维持能效的同时大幅缓解了计算精度退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器因其能实现高能效的模拟向量-矩阵乘法而在自然语言处理领域备受关注，但其计算过程存在权重编程和执行精度失真问题，导致性能下降。

Method: 识别出位置编码中较大输出值对ADC造成的压力，提出两种优化手段：1. 针对特定忆阻层调整权重与ADC精度位的比例；2. 在硬件配置不可调整时，移除编码相关的线性变换。

Result: 相比基准方案，优化权重与ADC位分配后的计算退化程度降低约50%；在无法修改ADC硬件的情况下，通过移除线性变换，退化程度仍可降低约30%。

Conclusion: 针对忆阻器计算中模数转换器（ADC）带来的性能退化，通过优化层级权重分布或精简线性变换，可以在保持能耗基本不变的情况下显著提升计算精度。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [237] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Web Agent模仿学习中专家干预时机难把握的问题，SRC框架提出通过固定视野的分支审查与回滚纠正，在减少过度依赖的同时高效过滤并利用高质量轨迹，提升模型学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web Agent通过模仿学习训练时，专家干预时机难以抉择：干预过晚会导致错误累积导致状态不可恢复，干预过早或过于频繁则导致Agent对专家策略过度依赖，陷入局部最优且缺乏泛化能力。

Method: 提出“投机回滚纠正”（Speculative Rollback Correction, SRC）框架：采用固定视野的分支审查（fixed-horizon branch review），仅在局部进度受损时由教师进行干预，保留有用轨迹前缀并进行回滚；通过“硬验证器”筛选成功轨迹，构建高质量轨迹库，最终利用这些数据对Agent进行下一步动作的监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity任务中，SRC成功收集了977条通过验证的完整轨迹和9,183个动作微调样本，显著改善了“恢复能力”与“查询开销”之间的权衡。

Conclusion: SRC 通过固定视野的“分支审查”机制和回滚策略，在平衡专家干预频率与Agent探索多样性方面优于传统的步级审查（Step-level review），不仅提升了在线交互式Web Agent的训练效率，还增强了模型从错误中恢复的能力。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [238] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化卡车预测性维护建模的方法，通过将时间序列转化为表格数据，结合AutoML技术，在降低维护成本的同时取得了优于现有SOTA方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队实施基于状态的预测性维护（PdM）虽能降低非计划停机和成本，但面临数据量巨大、故障检测算法复杂以及成本效益难以平衡的三大挑战。作者旨在建立一种简化、有效的PdM方法来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出了一种基于单调非递减时间序列假设的预测性维护方法。核心步骤是将传感器的时间序列数据截取最近观测值，并转化为表格格式，随后利用针对表格数据优化的机器学习（ML）模型和AutoML技术进行故障分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在降低维护成本方面优于当前的SOTA技术，同时有效降低了建模的技术门槛和复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法论在处理卡车车队预测性维护任务时，不仅相比现有技术（SOTA）降低了成本，还通过AutoML简化了建模流程，为解决大数据量与复杂故障检测问题提供了一个高效的方案。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [239] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Weibull分布对Transformer权重演化进行诊断，发现形状参数k与层的功能类别相关，尺度参数λ与训练进度相关，为理解深度学习模型训练动力学提供了通用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计指标无法有效解析Transformer权重分布的演变细节及跨模型架构的通用动力学规律。

Method: 将Weibull分布拟合于Transformer权重元素绝对值分布；通过对不同层、不同步骤的权重矩阵进行独立分布拟合，量化权重演化动态。

Result: 1. 传输类模块（FFN, W_o）的k值保持恒定（约1.20）；2. 选择类模块（W_q, W_k）的k值表现出架构依赖性（如GQA的影响）；3. λ值随训练过程稳步增长，并与训练超参数相关联。

Conclusion: Weibull分布的形状参数（k）能刻画神经网络层的函数类型，而尺度参数（λ）则能反映训练进度，提供了一种架构无关的诊断框架。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [240] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DynamicPTQ，通过分析残差流的阶段性动态跳变，在 W4A4KV4 量化场景下对敏感层采用动态混合精度（4/8-bit）策略，有效缓解了激活量化误差，提升了低比特 LLM 推理的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 W4A4KV4 低比特量化下，现有的基于变换的平滑方法（如正交旋转、仿射缩放）未能充分处理网络深层中残差流极值带来的跨层动态变化，导致量化误差放大。

Method: 1. 引入“跳变比（Jump Ratio）”和“历史特征信噪比（Historical Feature SNR）”量化指标分析残差流动力学；2. 提出 DynamicPTQ 策略，根据特征动态为敏感层分配不同的激活量化精度（混合 4/8-bit）；3. 将该机制集成于现有主流 PTQ 方法（如 QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant）。

Result: 在 LLaMA-2 和 LLaMA-3 模型上实现了更好的困惑度（Perplexity）及零样本 QA 性能；与纯 4-bit 量化相比，在保持极低显存开销的同时，吞吐量提升了 1.05 到 1.07 倍。

Conclusion: 本文指出静态平滑方法无法解决深层网络中随层变化的残差不稳定性。提出的 DynamicPTQ 通过在动态评估的敏感层使用 8-bit 量化，实现了在 W4A4KV4 场景下比纯静态方法更优的鲁棒性和性能折中。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [241] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证明了 PINNs 在药代动力学建模中的价值：不仅能匹配传统方法的精度，还能自动识别模型参数的不可辨识性，并灵活整合多种观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物学部分观测问题中，已知动力学方程但部分房室无法直接测量（如化疗药物的组织浓度），PINNs 可作为推断未观测状态的有效工具。作者旨在验证 PINNs 在该领域的表现及优势。

Method: 将物理信息神经网络（PINNs）应用于两房室药物动力学（PK）模型，并与非线性最小二乘法（NLS）和纯数据驱动的多层感知器（MLP）进行基准比较。测试场景包括线性模型和具有饱和消除特征的米氏动力学（Michaelis-Menten）模型，并评估了添加稀疏组织观测数据的影响。

Result: 在简单线性模型中，PINN 表现接近最优的 NLS，且能同步输出组织浓度；在米氏模型中，PINN 能识别模型的不可辨识性，且在引入少量组织观测后能显著提高参数恢复精度，表现优于无法处理此类非线性的 NLS 基准。

Conclusion: PINNs 提供了一种统一的框架，既能在经典问题上达到传统方法的性能，又能揭示传统方法无法识别的模型结构问题，并能灵活融合异构数据。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [242] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用 d-Simplex 固定分类器结合对比学习来学习平稳的兼容表征，从而解决模型更新时的兼容性问题，并显著提升了连续更新场景下的图像检索表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模模型更新维护中，重复处理检索库（gallery images）成本高昂。现有的表示学习方法难以在更新后的模型间保持特征兼容性，且仅依赖交叉熵损失难以捕捉特征空间的高阶依赖关系。

Method: 1. 使用 d-Simplex 固定分类器学习表征以确立兼容性基础；2. 结合交叉熵损失（Cross-Entropy Loss）与对比损失（Contrastive Loss）的凸组合（Convex Combination）来训练模型，以捕获更高阶的特征依赖；3. 在连续微调和模型零星替换的场景下进行实验验证。

Result: 提出的方法在保持表征平稳性的同时，无需重新处理库图像即可确保检索服务不中断，在性能上实现了最先进（SOTA）的表现。

Conclusion: 通过固定分类器（d-Simplex fixed classifiers）学习平稳表征是实现模型兼容性的有效手段。引入对比损失（Contrastive Loss）能够捕捉高级别依赖，进一步优化兼容性，并支持在模型连续微调及替换场景下实现无缝检索（无需重新处理库图像），达到当前最佳水平。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [243] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在监督域自适应中利用因果结构知识提升模型性能的理论界限，证明了其收益取决于候选预测器间的风险边界，并提出了在满足边界条件时能有效提升性能的聚合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在跨分布部署时性能通常会下降。尽管基于因果关系的域泛化（domain generalization）通常通过寻找不变特征来提升性能，但在有限样本环境（特别是监督域自适应 sDA）中，这些不变性到底能在多大程度上转化为性能增益尚不明确。

Method: 1. 以线性回归模型为框架，利用全量或部分因果结构信息指定不变或可能不变的特征子集；2. 导出候选预测器的目标风险界限；3. 提出一种自适应聚合程序；4. 推导出算法的有限样本上界和下界；5. 使用真实世界的因果基准测试验证理论。

Result: 1. 证明了有限样本下的增益受候选预测器间的风险边界与源域估计误差共同制约；2. 当目标风险边界足够大时，算法能匹配最佳候选预测器且避免负迁移；3. 建立了这些风险边界与线性结构因果模型（SCM）中结构偏移幅度的联系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了因果知识在监督域自适应（sDA）中提供有限样本收益的理论界限。研究表明，利用因果不变性提升模型性能的前提是候选预测器之间存在足够的“目标风险边界”（target-risk margins）。当这些边界足够大时，自适应聚合算法可以有效提升性能；当边界过小时，则无法获得有限样本速率的提升。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [244] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对RNN的抽象重构验证框架，通过SHAP引导的时间步关键性筛选，解决了非线性松弛误差在长序列中累积导致的验证保守性问题，显著提升了鲁棒性验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RNN鲁棒性验证方法中，非线性松弛带来的近似误差会随时间步推移而累积，导致验证结果过于保守，难以证明实际鲁棒的输入。

Method: 提出了一种抽象重构（Abstraction-Refinement）框架，通过分区（Partitioning）跨越零点的激活区间来消除非线性松弛误差，并结合SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）引导的时间步选择策略，仅对关键时间步进行重构，以控制计算复杂度。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST数据集上，该方法在验证成功率和鲁棒边界紧致性方面优于纯抽象基准方法，并明确了ReLU与tanh模型在运行时间上的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于SHAP的时间步选择的抽象重构框架有效提升了循环神经网络（RNN）局部鲁棒性验证的准确度和紧致性，克服了传统线性边界传播方法在长序列中累积误差过大的问题。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [245] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对从含噪数据中挑选最大值的问题，提出了一套兼具可容许性与“无妥协”特性的选择策略，并成功将其应用于随机优化，实现了比传统随机取样更优且具备理论底线的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅能通过单次无偏估计值 $y_i$ 来推断未知真实值 $x_i$ 的情况下，如何选择最优策略以提取最大值，同时保证算法在任何情况下都不逊色于基准算法。

Method: 通过设计同时满足可容许性且不劣于均匀随机选择（Uniform Random Selection）的决策策略，并将其应用于随机优化问题的在线转批量（online-to-batch conversion）转换中。

Result: 提出了一系列满足“无妥协（no-compromise）”性质的算法策略。在随机优化应用中，这些策略相比于传统的随机迭代选择具有更强的鲁棒性，且在理想条件下表现更佳。

Conclusion: 在选择最大值的场景中，存在同时满足“可容许性（admissibility）”和“不劣于基准策略（no-compromise）”的算法。该研究在随机优化领域通过“在线转批量”变换实现了这种保证，确保在最坏情况下不劣于随机取样，同时在良性环境下能获得显著的性能提升。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [246] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证明了两层线性自回归模型在训练后能自发习得卡尔曼滤波的行为，从而解释了该类模型为何能有效捕捉序列数据的潜在动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在序列数据处理中表现出色，但其学习潜在表示的能力和机理尚不明确，需要从理论上解释为何自动回归模型能够捕捉潜在动态。

Method: 1. 证明卡尔曼滤波器可以被具有有限截断误差的自回归模型良好近似；2. 分析该问题的非凸优化景观，证明所有驻点均为严格鞍点或全局最小值；3. 提供关于预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的有限样本理论保证。

Result: 理论证明表明，学习到的隐藏表示在相似变换下与最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计一致；数值模拟进一步验证了理论结果，证实了自回归模型能够有效恢复潜在状态估计。

Conclusion: 两层线性自回归模型通过经验风险最小化，在没有任何先验动力学知识的情况下，能够通过学习有效地逼近卡尔曼滤波，并恢复系统的潜在状态。该研究为理解自回归模型如何学习潜在表示提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [247] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Net-Ev²，一个用于模拟复杂网络中事件演化的生成式模拟器，通过拓扑感知扩散模型实现了对事件语义与网络结构演化的精准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式模拟器难以同时处理结构化属性与非结构化语义，且在模拟网络事件演化时无法有效捕捉复杂的网络拓扑结构。

Method: 提出了一个包含两个阶段的框架：1. 结构引导的掩码预训练；2. 拓扑感知扩散过程（通过基于图的 U-Net 下采样和上采样实现）。支持仅通过自然语言输入进行推理。

Result: 发布了包含四大交通网络、大规模多模态的基准数据集 Net-Ev²-6.5M，并提出了衡量拓扑保真度的新指标 JL-MMD。实验表明该方法在各项指标上均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: Net-Ev² 在模拟网络事件演化方面达到了最先进水平，表现出强大的拓扑保真度和泛化能力，为理解复杂网络中的扰动传播提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [248] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种极简的递归VLA架构——μVLA，通过在Transformer中引入少量可学习记忆Token进行跨时间步更新，在保持模型简洁性的同时，显著增强了VLA模型在部分可观测环境下的动作预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在部分可观测场景下表现不佳，而现有的记忆增强模型通过混合递归、检索、压缩等多种复杂机制，导致难以评估单纯“递归”机制对性能的贡献。因此，研究旨在通过受控隔离实验来解耦并验证递归机制本身的有效性。

Method: 提出μVLA模型，在预训练VLA骨干网络中引入一组跨时间步（timesteps）传递的可学习记忆Token。通过自注意力机制进行更新，并使用截断时间反向传播（TBPTT）进行端到端训练，没有添加任何辅助损失函数或架构性修改。研究通过控制变量法，系统对比了不同记忆宽度（m）、TBPTT长度（K）及更新规则（交叉步梯度与EMA）的效果。

Result: 在MIKASA-Robo基准上，μVLA将五项训练任务的平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84，并在未见任务上表现显著优于无记忆基线（0.23 vs 0.07）；在LIBERO完全可观测场景下，模型性能无衰减（96.2%成功率），证明了该方法的有效性和稳健性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将递归（Recurrence）以极简方式集成至VLA模型中，证明了在无需复杂架构改变的情况下，引入少量可学习的记忆Token即可显著提升模型处理部分可观测环境的能力。同时，该结果界定了“最小化骨干递归”的性能边界，指出了其适用性及仍需额外辅助记忆结构的场景。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [249] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了“可模拟过程”框架，证明利用数据生成过程的模拟器可以解决强依赖数据的学习问题，并在VC维度下实现了对传统独立数据PAC学习保证的复现，从而扩展了学习理论的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习理论大多依赖数据独立性假设，对强依赖数据的泛化保证研究不足。该研究旨在探索在处理复杂依赖数据时，实现统计学习泛化的最低假设条件。

Method: 引入了“可模拟过程（simulatable processes）”框架，通过访问能够近似数据生成分布的模拟器来克服数据依赖性；并利用时间受限的柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度（time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity）来控制遗憾（regret）。

Result: 1. 在模拟器辅助下，得出了依赖于VC维度的学习误差界；2. 证明了条件采样在统计效率和计算上的优势；3. 提出了一种通用算法，能够学习在多项式时间内可采样且具有可控时间受限柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度的任何VC类。

Conclusion: 提出“可模拟过程”框架，证明了在获取数据产生过程模拟器的情况下，即使面对强依赖数据，也能获得与独立同分布（i.i.d.）场景下同等级别的VC维度学习保证。该研究在学习理论中泛化了传统的PAC模型，并展示了条件采样在处理复杂数据依赖时的优越性。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [250] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究验证了机器学习驱动的微观仿真模型在交通冲突预测方面优于传统规则模型，展现了其无需特定地点校准即可提高事故预测精度的潜力，但在仿真碰撞细节的真实性上仍有待完善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于规则的微观仿真模型虽能模拟车流，但在重现现实冲突动态和提高事故预测精度方面存在局限。研究旨在探讨机器学习模型通过学习大规模轨迹数据来提升仿真真实性及事故预测能力的可行性。

Method: 以英国利兹的五个真实信号交叉口为研究对象，对比传统规则驱动模型（Rule-based）与最先进机器学习模型（ML-based）的交通微观仿真效果。利用二维碰撞时间（TTC）指标识别模拟冲突，并结合极值理论（Extreme Value Theory）预测事故频率，最后与真实事故数据进行比对分析。

Result: 对比实验显示，基于机器学习模型生成的冲突数据所预测的事故频率与现实数据吻合度更高，且无需针对特定路口进行复杂的模型校准；相比之下，规则模型由于缺乏针对性校准，无法提供有意义的预测结果。此外，直接使用机器学习仿真生成的碰撞事故数据预测现实事故表现不佳，说明模型在仿真事故细节方面仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的驾驶行为模型在交通冲突预测方面具有显著潜力，且无需进行繁琐的特定地点模型校准。然而，目前该模型尚不能直接生成符合现实的碰撞事故，未来的研究仍需在提高事故生成真实性方面进行深入探讨。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [251] [M*: A Modular, Extensible, Serving System for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12688)
*Atindra Jha,Naomi Sagan,Keisuke Kamahori,Irmak Sivgin,Rohan Sanda,Steven Gao,Mark Horowitz,Luke Zettlemoyer,Olivia Hsu,Jure Leskovec,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 M*，一个通用的复合 AI 模型服务系统。通过将模型建模为“Walk Graph”数据流图，M* 解决了现有 Serving 框架无法支持复杂多模态组件组合的问题，实现了跨任务的高效推理优化与部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代复合模型架构（集成视觉、语言、扩散、语音、动作生成等）日益复杂，而现有的模型服务框架基于过窄的模型结构假设，无法有效支撑这些新型架构的部署需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Walk Graph”的模块化抽象方式，将模型表示为数据流图，以处理跨模态的任务；利用该抽象实现组件的任意组合、灵活的物理算力部署及模型中立的分布式优化。

Result: 在文本到图像任务中，比 vLLM-Omni 平均延迟降低 20%；在文本到语音任务中，实时因子降低至 2.9 倍，吞吐量提升至 2.7 倍；在机器人规划任务中，比 V-JEPA 2-AC 高出 12.5 倍的性能。

Conclusion: M* 通过引入 Walk Graph 抽象，提供了一个支持多种复合模型架构的通用服务框架，实现了更高效率的推理服务，解决了现有 Serving 框架无法适应新型多模态融合模型架构的痛点。

Abstract: We are entering a new era of composite model architectures that integrate diverse components such as vision encoders, language backbones, diffusion and flow heads, audio codecs, action generators, and world-model predictors. Such architectures underpin a broad class of multimodal models, including unified multimodal models, omni models, speech-language models, vision-language-action policies, and world models. However, existing model serving frameworks were built on narrow assumptions about model structure, making them ill-suited to accommodate this new architectural diversity. Here we present M*, a universal serving system for efficient serving of composite AI models. M* represents models as dataflow graphs, processing requests spanning diverse modalities and tasks as traversals over these graphs. The core insight is a modular abstraction that supports arbitrary composition of model components, flexible placement onto a physical cluster, and model-agnostic optimizations within a distributed runtime. We call this abstraction the Walk Graph and show how it can concisely capture composite models from a broad range of families. We instantiate M* on representative models and find that it achieves, on average, 20% lower end-to-end latency than vLLM-Omni for text-to-image workloads on BAGEL, while delivering up to 2.9x lower real-time factor and 2.7x higher throughput for text-to-speech workloads on Qwen3-Omni. M* also outperforms the V-JEPA 2-AC rollout baseline for robotic planning by up to 12.5x. Thus, our work paves the road towards more efficient serving of complex models with minimal developer effort.

</details>


### [252] [Reliability of Probabilistic Emulation of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12997)
*Sam F. Greenbury,Radka Jersakova,Paolo Conti,Marjan Famili,Christopher Iliffe Sprague,Edwin Brown,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了物理系统预报中两大主流方法——生成式模型与CRPS训练集成法，发现CRPS集成法在可靠性、推理速度和对潜空间的适应性上表现更优，并提供了开源实现框架及数据集工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前物理系统概率预测的两大主流方法（生成式模型与CRPS集成法）在准确性上表现良好，但缺乏对其不确定性（可靠性）的系统性评估与对比，且对其在不同空间（潜空间与环境空间）下的表现差异认知不足。

Method: 构建了一个统一的评估框架，在相同模型规模和计算预算下，横向对比了生成式模型（扩散/流匹配）与CRPS训练的集成方法；在多种2D时空物理系统上进行单步预测和自回归滚动测试，并考察经验覆盖率、准确性及推理效率。

Result: 1. CRPS集成法在单步和自回归预测中均表现出更可靠的不确定性估计（更好的覆盖率）。2. 生成式模型仅在环境空间下才能达到与CRPS相当的覆盖率，但推理延迟高。3. CRPS集成法在潜空间下效果稳定，且推理速度显著快于生成式模型。4. 两类方法在预测准确性上旗鼓相当。

Conclusion: 在物理系统的概率预报中，CRPS训练的集成方法在不确定性可靠性（覆盖率）和推理效率上均优于生成式模型；尽管生成式模型在环境空间中能表现出可比的可靠性，但其计算代价巨大。此外，CRPS集成法对潜空间的适应性较强，且研究发布了模块化框架AutoCast和数据集工具AutoSim。

Abstract: Two dominant approaches have emerged for generating probabilistic forecasts of physical systems: generative models, such as diffusion or flow matching; and ensembles of deterministic models with stochasticity injected, trained using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. While both approaches have demonstrated strong predictive accuracy, the reliability of their uncertainties has not been systematically assessed. We address this gap by developing a framework to evaluate both approaches across diverse 2D spatiotemporal physical systems, under matched model size and computational budget. We assess the reliability of probabilistic emulation by inspecting the empirical coverage of predictive intervals, while also considering accuracy and computational efficiency metrics. CRPS-trained ensembles typically achieve more reliable uncertainties on both single-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts, demonstrating better coverage than the standard alternative of training generative models in a latent space. Moreover, the CRPS approach offers significantly faster inference. When generative models are trained in ambient rather than a compressed latent space, which is often infeasible for high-dimensional problems, they exhibit comparable coverage to CRPS-trained ensembles, though with substantially larger inference latency. In contrast, when CRPS-trained ensembles are trained in latent space they do not show a marked degradation in coverage with respect to ambient space. Both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles demonstrate good predictive accuracy. To facilitate future research and application, we release AutoCast, a modular framework implementing both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles, alongside AutoSim, a flexible dataset generation package for rapid prototyping.

</details>


### [253] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conformal QoT框架，通过引入一致性预测技术解决光网络中QoT估计的领域偏移问题，将预测准确率显著提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QoT（传输质量）估计模型在面对实际网络环境中的领域偏移（即训练数据与测试数据分布不一致）时，往往导致预测准确率下降，无法满足可靠性要求。

Method: 提出了一种结合一致性预测（Conformal Prediction）与操作决策策略（Operational Decision Policies）的新型框架。该方法利用统计保证的QoT估计来应对数据分布变化，从而提高预测系统的鲁棒性。

Result: 在公开数据集上的测试表明，该框架将预测准确率从92%提升至99.6%，证明了该方法在处理复杂网络环境下的优越性。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架通过将一致性预测（Conformal Prediction）引入QoT估计，成功解决了领域偏移（Domain Shift）带来的模型泛化能力差的问题，实现了高置信度的光通路可行性预测。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [254] [Towards More General Control of Diffusion Models Using Jeffrey Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13240)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Jes Frellsen,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Jeffrey Guidance，这是一种运用统计学条件化规则对扩散模型采样进行精细控制的新框架，不仅提升了生成图像的质量，还解决了公平性约束等复杂控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的扩散模型引导技术（Guidance）在处理隐式目标分布或复杂约束时的局限性，旨在提供一种更普适、更具原则性的控制方法。

Method: 引入基于“杰弗里条件化规则”（Jeffrey's rule of conditioning）的引导框架，旨在更新边缘分布以匹配特定目标分布，同时最小化对原始联合分布的干扰。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 和 FFHQ 数据集上显著降低了 FID 指标；在 CelebA-HQ 上成功应用于实现属性独立性，提升了模型的公平性。

Conclusion: Jeffrey Guidance 提供了一种原则性的控制框架，通过杰弗里条件化规则（Jeffrey's rule of conditioning）实现对扩散模型输出的精确控制，证明了其在图像质量提升和公平性保障方面的有效性。

Abstract: A key strength of diffusion models lies in their flexibility, since their outputs can be controlled at sampling time through guidance. However, beyond simple cases such as conditional sampling, the target distribution is often left implicit, defined only through a sampling rule or a heuristic energy function. To address this, we propose Jeffrey guidance, a principled framework that extends diffusion-model control to applications beyond what standard guidance can express. It leverages Jeffrey's rule of conditioning to update marginal distributions towards a prescribed target, preserving the conditional structure and minimally perturbing the joint distribution. We first demonstrate Jeffrey guidance by targeting a prescribed embedding distribution. With Inception embeddings as the target, this leads to substantial reductions in FID on both CIFAR-10 and FFHQ. We further apply Jeffrey guidance to fairness on CelebA-HQ, updating an unconditional diffusion model to enforce independence between attributes.

</details>


### [255] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dolph2Vec，这是首个针对海豚的物种特定自监督学习模型。实验证明，该模型在海豚通信任务中表现出色，且能自动提取具有生物学意义的细粒度声学单元，为动物交流研究提供了新的科学工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用SSL模型在生物声学领域侧重于跨物种泛化，无法满足对单一物种复杂交流系统进行细粒度分析的需求，因此需要一个专门针对海豚声学特征优化的高性能模型。

Method: 1. 数据集：收集并发布了包含5只海豚、跨度超过5年的纵向录音数据集。2. 模型：基于Wav2Vec 2.0架构，定制化训练了名为“Dolph2Vec”的物种特定SSL模型。3. 任务：在签名哨音分类和哨音检测两个生物学相关任务上进行基准测试。

Result: Dolph2Vec在签名哨音分类和哨音检测任务上均显著优于通用基准模型；此外，模型习得的嵌入和码本空间能够捕捉到与海豚哨音分类相符甚至更细致的可解释声学单元。

Conclusion: Dolph2Vec验证了将自监督学习（SSL）应用于特定物种的有效性，证明了该方法不仅能提升下游任务性能，还能作为解析复杂动物交流系统底层结构的科学工具。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>
